# SMH Cycle v0: the post-print clock, the return lanes, and the actuarial table - proof given a measured timeline

Status: DESIGN BOOKKEEPING on the UNSEALED pre-2021 sample only (rule-set-V class). Date filter date < 2021-01-01 throughout; holdout 2021+ SPENT and never touched. Built 12 Nov 2026, session 42, PROGRESS block 50, on Andrea's "continue developing the hypothesis and proving it". Nothing here is an inferential claim, a test, or a trade; the forward protocol is unchanged. All constants below are recomputed and asserted by the smh_theorem.py selftest (verify_all's smh_theorem check; the bundle stays at 20 checks).

This layer answers the two questions the aftermath (docs/smh_aftermath_v0.md) left open, and then does the one thing that can honestly be done with the word "proof" on a design sample: it measures the TIMELINE of proof.

On proof, stated once and frozen: nothing computable on the 2000-2020 sample can prove the hypothesis - every statistic on that sample is bookkeeping by definition, and the holdout is spent. The proof object of this framework is the forward calibration record: rows logged at clock entries before outcomes, scored against outcomes, dying at the pre-declared kill lines. What design data CAN do is say how long proof takes. That is this layer's third table.

## 1. The post-print clock (KM to the NEXT significant week)

Kaplan-Meier from each significant week to the next significant week, censoring at the sample end. Two versions: from all 47 pre-2021 significant weeks, and from the 26 first events of compression entries specifically.

| clock | cum h4 | cum h12 (Greenwood 95%) | cum h26 | median |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| from any of the 47 prints | 0.149 | 0.299 [0.168, 0.430] | 0.650 [0.512, 0.788] | 19 weeks |
| from the 26 first events | 0.038 [0.000, 0.112] | 0.231 [0.069, 0.393] | 0.654 [0.471, 0.837] | 18 weeks |

Readings, frozen:

1. THE SUPPRESSION, SEPARATED. From the 26 first events, the probability of another print within 12 weeks is 0.231 [0.069, 0.393] - BELOW the climatology counterfactual 0.441 [0.411, 0.471] with disjoint bands. After an entry-conditioned print, the next print within the quarter is rarer than from a week picked at random. This is the lone-print finding (aftermath section 1) restated as a clock and carried against its counterfactual; the mirror image of the salience gradient (entry clocks above climatology at the cross, below it after the print). Caveats frozen: n = 26 starts, episode dependence everywhere, and the ENDOGENOUS BAR - the print raises sig_w (median 1.324x), so part of the suppression is mechanical; an absolute-vol yardstick is a different definition. Suggestive, mechanically assisted, stated with its bands.
2. THE QUIET AFTER THE DRAMA. Zero prints in the first 3 weeks after a first event (0 of 26; from any print the count is 6 of 47). The quiet-handoff pattern repeats at the event's exit.
3. PRINTS RECUR ONCE THE BAR SETTLES. By 26 weeks the cum reaches 0.650/0.654: the machine prints again within about half a year with two-thirds probability. The observer who just caught a print is not done observing; the observer waiting for the NEXT one waits a median 18-19 weeks.

## 2. The return lanes (how the machine comes home)

Of the 26 first events, the road to the next compression cross (1 censored at the sample end) splits:

| lane | count | median event-to-cross wait |
|---|---|---|
| DECAY-FIRST (cross before any fresh stress entry) | 14 | 11.5 weeks |
| STRESS-FIRST (a fresh stress entry intervenes) | 11 | 20.0 weeks |

The faster lane is the quiet one: when the machine slides home on vol decay alone it arrives in about 11 and a half weeks; when it goes through a fresh stress spell first, the trip takes nearly twice as long. The circle has two lanes and the boring lane is faster - the doctrine's erosion-not-catharsis finding in path form.

## 3. The actuarial table (the long-run lemma quantified)

In-sample empirical rates over the 1050 valid weeks (20.12 years). STATIONARITY IS NOT CLAIMED: the sample spans the dot-com bust and the GFC; the live rate belongs to the forward log, not to this table.

| quantity | design rate |
|---|---|
| compression entries (triggers) | 41 in 20.12 years = 2.04 per year, ~20.4 per decade |
| entry-conditioned first events | 26 in 20.12 years = 1.29 per year, ~12.9 per decade |
| significant weeks | 47 in 20.12 years = 2.34 per year, ~23.4 per decade |
| mean cycle between compression entries | 1050 / 41 = 25.6 weeks |
| region occupancy | compression 27.5% / mid 30.6% / stress 41.9% |

TILING CONSISTENCY ANCHOR (new standing check): the three frozen dwell tables tile the sample exactly - 41 spells x mean 7.05 + 66 x 4.86 + 33 x 13.33 = 1049.7 ~= 1050 = the raw occupancy count. The dwell tables, measured independently in session 35, sum to the whole calendar with no remainder. The selftest asserts this.

The actuarial chain: 2.04 triggers per year x 0.634 capture = 1.29 events per year, internally consistent with the direct count (26/20.12). The long-run lemma is now a QUANTITY: about one and a quarter entry-conditioned anticipated events per year across the cycle, about thirteen per decade, none of them dated.

## 4. The proof timeline (the honest answer to "proving it")

At the design trigger rate, the pre-declared kill-line gates arrive at:

| gate | scored rows needed | years at 2.04 triggers/year |
|---|---|---|
| first public gate | n = 10 | ~4.9 |
| | n = 20 | ~9.8 |
| | n = 40 | ~19.6 |
| | n = 60 | ~29.4 |

Readings, frozen:

1. PROOF IS FORWARD AND IT IS SLOW. The occurrence claim cannot die (or survive) in public for roughly five years; the decisive gates (20-40 rows) sit at 10-20 years. This is the measured cost of doing science on a market clock that triggers twice a year. Anyone demanding faster proof is demanding a different, dishonest machine.
2. THE RATE IS A DESIGN NUMBER, NOT A PROMISE. If the live trigger rate differs (regime drift, structural vol change), the timeline stretches or shrinks with it; the forward log measures the live rate as it accumulates. The kill lines themselves are row-counted, never clock-counted: the gates open when the rows exist, not when the calendar says so.
3. WHAT ACCELERATES NOTHING: more design statistics. Every additional table on 2000-2020 data moves nothing about proof; the design side is complete.

## 5. Caveats, frozen

1. n = 47 starts (all prints) and n = 26 (first events); every band above carries episode dependence; the KM treats overlapping episodes as independent and they are not.
2. The suppression is mechanically assisted by the endogenous 2.5 x sig_w bar (the print raises its own threshold); flagged beside the claim, and the disjoint-band statement is made only for the first-events clock, not the all-prints clock (whose band overlaps climatology's marginally: 0.430 vs 0.411).
3. The actuarial rates are in-sample empirical over a specific 20-year window; no stationarity, no projection, no forward content.
4. The kill-year column is arithmetic on the design rate (n / 2.04), not a forecast of when gates open.
5. No rule, probability, kill line, or protocol value changed in this layer; T, V, V4h untouched; nothing booked; the forward log stays empty by design.

## 6. Honest computation log

- The first scratch pass at the post-print Greenwood bands used (1 - S) where the SE of the cumulative needs S: the bands came out absurdly narrow ([0.131, 0.167] at h4 where the correct band is [0.047, 0.251]). Caught by sanity-checking the width against the at-risk counts before any constant was frozen; recomputed with the standard Greenwood form S * sqrt(sum d/(n(n-d))) used throughout the bundle; all constants above are from the corrected pass. The selftest now asserts the corrected values on every run.
- The quiet-3w constant (0 of 26) was cross-checked against the raw inter-arrival gaps (only 2 of 46 gaps are consecutive) before freezing.

# SMH DOSSIER v0 — the unifying clue dossier, criteria ordered by closeness to the anticipated event

Status: CONSOLIDATION DOCUMENT (18 Nov 2026, session 94, PROGRESS block 97), answering the owner's 18 Nov 2026 directive: "look historically all we saw and start building criteria list by closeness to actual event starting from price and then volume and then time to build a unifying clue dossier that gives enough to form a whole framework once."

Documentation only. Every number below is a frozen, already-registered design constant restated from the cited bundle documents; no new computation was performed, nothing was evaluated, nothing was booked, no sealed window was touched (NQ 2021+ holdout SPENT; cross-market 2021+ sealed; BTC 2021+ sealed). All design statistics are bookkeeping on the unsealed pre-2021 sample and never proof; the proof object is the forward calibration record alone, which is empty by design. Direction is refused everywhere in this dossier; nothing below predicts a sign.

## 0. The end goal, restated formally

The framework the owner wants is exactly four questions answered at a decision close:
1. WHEN is a big move likely? (the hazard)
2. HOW CLOSE in time can we get to it? (the timeliness margin)
3. WHICH WAY? (direction — refused until forward rows earn it)
4. WHAT DOES IT COST to be there? (exploitability — outside every registered claim)

The dossier assembles every measured clue the programme holds, ordered by closeness to the event within the owner's requested layering: price first, then volume, then time. Sections marked NULL are clues that were tested honestly and measured to carry nothing; they stay in the dossier because a unifying framework is defined as much by what it excludes as by what it includes.

## 1. PRICE LAYER (clues measured at the decision close — closeness: the trigger itself)

### 1.1 The compression cross — the programme's strongest clue
- Definition (frozen): sig_w, the weekly volatility measure, crosses down into the frozen compression region. One trigger, one event, one clock.
- The anticipated event E: a significant week, |r1| >= 2.5 x sig_w, within 12 weeks.
- Measured occurrence: 0.634 (26/41 design windows), Greenwood band [0.487, 0.782], versus do-nothing climatology 0.441. Bands disjoint. GAP +0.193. (smh_hazard_v0, smh_proof_criteria_v0)
- Robustness: positive in every cell of the 11-cell definition-elasticity audit (gaps 0.106-0.222), and the frozen definition is deliberately NOT the grid optimum. (smh_robustness_v0, family_registry_v0)
- Cross-market, same rank-transferred rule, zero re-tuning (design windows): GOLD cum12 0.617 [0.478, 0.756] vs 0.481, gap +0.136; NIKKEI 0.562 [0.422, 0.703] vs 0.374, gap +0.188; DAX 0.680 [0.551, 0.809] vs 0.465, gap +0.215. The gap sign is positive in every market tested, within the NQ grid's own range. (smh_crossmarket_stageA_v0)
- Kill criteria: 3/8/19/31 events at 10/20/40/60 scored forward rows. (smh_proof_criteria_v0)

### 1.2 The stress trigger — the second, weaker clue
- Same construction on the other side: cross into the stress region. Design p0 0.636 (21/33), the SAME kill-line thresholds (round to the same 5% binomial quantile). Recorded honestly as the weaker clue: its edge over a coin is smaller than compression's edge over climatology. (smh_proof_criteria_v0)

### 1.3 Event anatomy — what the anticipated move actually is
- Median lag from the entry to the event: 5 weeks.
- Median overshoot of the event bar: 1.41x its own 2.5-sigma threshold; maximum 2.63x. The median significant week is a ~3.5-sigma week. "Anticipated big move" concretely means this: a threshold-crossing event of measured central size, not a point target.
- Median dwell of the compression spell: 4 weeks. The entry-anchored clock and the spell-anchored abort condition were reconciled by the competing-risks decomposition. (smh_event_anatomy_v0)

### 1.4 Timeliness — the ranked closeness ladder (weeks of forfeited capture)
- Enter at the cross: 0.634.
- Wait 4 quiet weeks, then enter: 0.500.
- Wait 6 quiet weeks: 0.348 (= (0.634 - 0.439)/(1 - 0.439)).
- Waiting gains nothing the clock does not already condition on, forfeits the front-loaded mass, and races the spell's own mortality (median dwell 4). THE COMPRESSION CROSS IS THE MEASURED CLOSEST APPROACH TO THE EVENT. Finer timing needs intraday data the programme does not hold. (smh_entry_theorem_v0, smh_methodology_review_v0)

### 1.5 Priced-ness — the clue's cheapness, measured and frozen
- Implied vol (VXN) at compression entries: median 16.47 versus 20.14 unconditional; the 25.8th percentile of its own distribution (mid entries the 46.3rd, stress entries the 67.4th; region occupancy 22.5 / 42.4 / 77.7). Level-cheap.
- But MEAN-priced: the variance risk premium at entries is +3.71 vol points (positive at 73.2% of entries) versus +4.55 (76.7%) unconditional. The strong unseen-ness claim — the market fails to price the coming expansion — is REFUTED at the mean in the design sample. The crowd's calm is still overpriced calm. (smh_unseen_v0; smh_priced_calm_v0 honesty retained: era confound severe, n = 21/20 across two decades)

## 2. VOLUME LAYER (the honest null layer — what volume did and did not deliver)

### 2.1 NULLS — carried in the dossier as exclusions
- H8 flow-fade (divTop), Amendment 3 signed: n = 32, wrong-signed, p 0.79. Volume divergence does not call tops in the future tense. (amendment3_h8_h9_topbottom)
- H9 climax: n = 3 events in 26 years. Too rare to be a clue. divBot: 0 events. (same)
- sp_z at-entry covariate: null, as pre-declared. The one prior sp_z entry-timing filter produced zero trades ever. (smh_spz_covariate_v0)
- AVFI in-sample direction path: 0.42 versus random-entry 0.465. The OHLCV-derived direction guess is not directional evidence. (smh_strategy_v0)
- Second-order VPVI flow (geometry proxy, session 92): not flow, close-free, reversal bars misread by construction. Display machinery only.
- Holdout battery: rule set T failed at the margin (0.575, p 0.069 vs 0.05); U3 failed too (0.418, p 0.116). Twenty-seven indicator variants dead in the H-number era.

### 2.2 What volume DOES carry, registered
- AVFI as a slow regime descriptor and sizing throttle — the armed-sizing verdict. Volume's measured job is exposure control, not entry timing and not direction.
- UNTESTED AND DATA-BLOCKED: the volume/Hawkes layer needs the broker/CME 1999-2000 EOD export (Amendment 10, decision + export together); the options term-structure unseen-ness layer needs his data-source choice (Amendment 14 candidate, unsigned). These are the volume layer's two live levers, both waiting on the owner.

### 2.3 Bookkeeping tracks (certify fidelity, never an edge)
- T: weekly paper, Entry 1 booked, FLAT since 2026-10-30; next decision close Friday 2026-11-20.
- V4h: 11 booked rows, equity 99,092.88, audit CLEAN; no booking since Friday 2026-11-13 (the standing decay item, his task-system check).
- Amendment 9 macro booking: rehearsed green, never fired; first fire window Sunday 2026-11-22 23:45 Malta.

## 3. TIME LAYER (the calendar layer — actuarial, not dated prophecy)

### 3.1 Trigger actuarial
- ~2.04 triggers per year, ~1.29 anticipated events/year. ROWS ARE STABLE, YEARS ARE NOT: capture fraction 0.647 vs 0.625 across decade halves, while the trigger rate moves by a factor of 4.5. Regime range 0.7-3.2 triggers/year. Hot regime: first gate in ~3 years; cold: nearer 15. The kill lines are row-counted, never clock-counted. (smh_priced_calm_v0, smh_cycle_v0)

### 3.2 Proof schedule (the falsification spine of the whole framework)
- Compression kill lines: 3/8/19/31 events at 10/20/40/60 scored rows; false-kill (true 0.634 machine) 0.0335/0.0282/0.0290/0.0415; power against the measured climatology 0.2855/0.4465/0.7243/0.9046 (the falsificationist's fee is ~3-4% per gate; the cumulative one-run-in-eight true-machine death ~0.126 is the stated price of public falsifiability).
- Occurrence gates arrive roughly 5/10/20/29 years at the design rate; the readout schedule ~13/25/51 years.
- THE POSITIVE PROOF is exactly one statement: at least 57 of 104 resolved compression 12-week windows resolve with the event. Power 0.971, false-pass 0.018.
- Direction peeks (pre-declared, refused until then): n = 40 and n = 60 scored rows, roughly ~20 and ~29 years. (smh_proof_criteria_v0, smh_methodology_review_v0)

### 3.3 Cycle vocabulary — descriptive only
- The Copsey cycle ladders (82/164-week, 24/48-week, 196-month, 33-year): hypothesis generator for the parked H3; zero statistical evidence in that source, no nulls, no counts. In the dossier as vocabulary, never as a clue.

## 4. THE GAPS, ranked by what stands between the dossier and his end goal
1. DIRECTION. The largest gap, refused as a theorem. The design sample is spent; any direction rule built on it now is curve-fit wearing a costume. The two peeks are the only lawful path (~20/~29 years), unless a signed amendment brings NEW data types whose directional content can be tested on sealed windows.
2. FORWARD PROOF. Zero scored rows. The first gate is the binding next milestone on the road; the macro cross-market stream is the only instrument that can open gates before NQ's own clock.
3. PRICED-NESS. Level-cheap but mean-priced; whether the options layer refines this is exactly Amendment 14's question (his a/b/c data-source choice).
4. COST. Post-cost exploitability is outside every claim. No position-sizing claim in this dossier is tradeable advice; paper only.
5. INTRADAY TIMING. Closer-than-weekly entry needs L2/intraday data; the runbook exists, the download is his hands.

## 5. What can compress this calendar (all owner-side)
The decision queue (docs/decision_queue_v0.md) in suggested order: the C-W3 barrier-repair ratification; Amendment 13 (C-W1 BTC weekly); Amendment 14 (D-3 options unseen-ness) with the data-source choice; D-1; D-6; Amendment 10 with the broker/CME export; shelfed 6/7/8. Hands-only: the L2 download, the V4h task check, the TradingView paste cross-check, the export checklist 093.

## 6. What this dossier is
A unifying framework exists in this dossier as a falsification architecture: one price trigger per side with measured hazards and kill lines, a measured timeliness ladder, an honest volume-null layer, an actuarial time layer, and a public proof statement. It is not a direction system, and by the central theorem it cannot become one by looking harder at the same spent history. What it can do, and only forward time or the owner's new data can grant, is earn the missing layers one scored row at a time — starting with the first gate, and for direction, the first peek.

# SMH Edge Route v0: how edge will be found, and the cross-market mould

Status: DEVELOPMENT DOCUMENT. This is the written answer to the standing question "how will edge actually be found; how does SMH mould into prediction by inevitabilities criteria it uniquely achieves, applicable across all markets." It is a design statement grounded in the frozen protocol and the now-measured Stage A cross-market bookkeeping (docs/smh_crossmarket_stageA_v0.md, GOLD/NIKKEI/DAX gaps +0.136/+0.188/+0.215 against frozen NQ +0.193). It creates no permission, no claim, and no evaluation; every number it cites is design bookkeeping or a frozen constant.

## 1. The core commitment: edge is actuarial, not oracular

SMH does not predict prices. It predicts a recurrence frequency: given a compression entry (or stress entry) on a market's own clock, the probability that a first significant week lands within 12 weeks is above that market's matched climatology by a frozen margin. The prediction object is a HAZARD, not a level and not a direction. Edge is found if and only if forward booked rows, scored against each market's own pre-frozen kill lines and climatology, survive. That is an actuarial edge: it monetizes like an insurance book (many small calibrated bets on event timing), not like a forecast of where the market goes.

The "inevitability" is not a claim that the event must happen. It is a claim about the RELIABILITY OF RECURRENCE STATISTICS under a definition that does not need retuning per market: compression episodes are followed by significant weeks at rates materially above unconditional climatology, in every market tested at design stage so far, under cuts transferred by rank with zero re-tuning. What is "inevitable" is the shape of the conditional clock, not any single event.

## 2. The inevitability criteria SMH uniquely achieves, and why they transfer across markets

The hypothesis was built to satisfy five criteria simultaneously; each one is what makes the cross-market mould honest rather than curve-fit:

1. ACTUARIAL RECURRENCE. The claim is a frequency ratio (entry cum12 vs matched climatology cum12) with pre-frozen kill lines, evaluable at small n, one market at a time. It does not need a long profitable track record to be falsified; ten forward rows can already breach a kill line (GOLD n=10 line is 3; NIKKEI is 2). Most market hypotheses cannot state a death schedule in advance; SMH's is public before the first row.
2. EDGE-LOCAL SALIENCE GAP. The audited quantity is the GAP, not the level: entry-anchored event frequency minus climatology. The gap is the one quantity the NQ definition-grid audits showed robust (it survived the definition grid; naive band-overlap did not survive rival constructions). Stage A shows the gap sign is positive in GOLD, NIKKEI and DAX at magnitudes inside the NQ grid's own cell range. A level would be market-specific; a gap is portable because it is measured against each market's own unconditional baseline, whatever that baseline is.
3. HAZARD TIMING, NOT POINT FORECASTS. The prediction is a survival curve (front-loaded hazards, median lag 5-6 weeks, cum4/cum12 split), which is tradeable information for option buyers, calendar spreaders, and risk managers even with direction refused. Timing claims are also cheap to falsify: the 12-week window closes mechanically, censored rows included, no discretion.
4. DIRECTION REFUSAL. SMH deliberately refuses to predict direction on design data (direction is forward-only, two peeks per market, registered in advance). This is what makes the design stage honest: no cell of the cross-market design was selected for direction performance, so nothing is curve-fit to up/down asymmetry. It also forces the edge to live in timing and calibration, where the actuarial structure is strongest.
5. PUBLIC FALSIFIABILITY WITH PRE-FROZEN CONSTANTS. Cuts by rank, kill lines, climatology bands, p0 values, holdout boundaries: all frozen in dated signed documents before the first forward row, per market. Cross-market, the family max-t rule is declared in advance. No other market hypothesis Andrea works with has this property; it is the anti-overfitting criterion, and Stage A honored it (lines frozen in the addendum the same day the results were computed, before any forward row exists anywhere).

The phrase "criteria it uniquely achieves" is thus operationally precise: the five criteria are simultaneously satisfiable by an actuarial recurrence claim and are NOT satisfiable by a direction-forecast or pattern-recognition claim (which cannot pre-freeze a death schedule, cannot refuse direction, and cannot transfer across markets without re-tuning). That is the mould.

## 3. How the definition transfers: the cross-market mould

1. RANK TRANSFER, NOT VALUE TRANSFER. A new market gets cuts at the same PERCENTILES of its own pre-2021 sig_w distribution (27.52nd / 58.10th), not the NQ values. This is why "same recipe" is not "same parameters": GOLD's LOW cut is 0.0160, NIKKEI's 0.0196, DAX's 0.0193; each is that market's own rank location.
2. PER-MARKET EVERYTHING. Each market gets its own Stage A (design bookkeeping on its own unsealed pre-2021 window), its own sealed 2021+ holdout, its own climatology, its own kill lines (GOLD 3/8/19/30, NIKKEI 2/7/16/26, DAX 3/9/21/34 at n=10/20/40/60), its own two direction peeks, its own forward log. Nothing pools.
3. FAMILY MULTIPLICITY CONTROL. Any claim stated across markets ("positive gap in all four") is evaluated under the family max-t across all four; design statistics are bookkeeping, never proof, everywhere.
4. THE FORWARD LOG IS THE PROOF SURFACE. Design windows can never prove anything (amendment Section 6); they exist to freeze constants and to make sure the mechanism is not instrument-specific BEFORE asking for forward rows. The mould is: Stage 0x occupancy, Stage A bookkeeping + kill-line freeze, then forward rows at clock entries, scored against the frozen schedule.

## 4. How edge will actually be found: the route, step by step

STEP 1 (DONE THIS SESSION): machinery validated (NQ parity gate passed first run, determinism verified), Stage A bookkeeping on GOLD/NIKKEI/DAX, kill lines frozen per market in the addendum. The mechanism's sign, timing shape and gap magnitude replicate at design stage; the question is now purely forward.
STEP 2 (NEXT BUILD): cross-market forward-booking tooling, mirroring the NQ forecast/score scripts with per-market cuts, logs and kill lines. Each Sunday, each market's clock either fires an entry (a row is booked with direction refused) or does not. Logs start empty BY DESIGN.
STEP 3 (ACCUMULATION): rows accumulate only at the market's own clock speed. With ~41-50 entries per decade per market at design density, four markets book roughly 4 rows per year in total (this is a slow actuarial book; the 10-20 h/week cap is not the constraint, the clock is).
STEP 4 (SCORING): at each n anchor (10/20/40/60), each market's arm either survives or breaches its kill line and closes UNTESTED-FAILED-DESIGN (the design cum12 was wrong about that market). Censored rows count as non-events, no exceptions. Two direction peeks per market, registered in advance, tell whether the timing edge carries an up/down asymmetry worth a second, separately-amended claim; the peeks are diagnostics, never entry rules.
STEP 5 (THE EDGE ITSELF): if arms survive, the durable private asset is the CALIBRATION RECORD: a multi-market book of survival curves with pre-frozen constants and a public falsification history. That record is what a systematic options program (buying 4-to-12-week premium at compression entries), a risk desk (pre-positioning for regime transitions), or a prop calendar-spreading book consumes. The tradable expression of an actuarial timing edge is optionality, not futures direction.
STEP 6 (ACCELERATION IS REPLICATION, NOT TUNING): the only honest way to get more evidence faster is more markets under the same mould (Amendment 12 draft: ES, ZN, DX, BTC with the claims-ledger C-W1 resolution and the 24/7 calendar caveat), each paying its own Stage A and its own kill lines. Re-tuning a failing market's constants is forbidden by the protocol and would destroy the falsifiability criterion that makes the edge credible to any counterparty.

## 5. What edge will NOT be (negative commitments, standing)

1. No new entry rules mined from design data (design statistics are bookkeeping; a seductive cell in any market's design window is a trap, not a strategy).
2. No direction strategy until the registered peeks say so under their own future amendment; direction refusal is load-bearing for honesty, not a limitation to optimize away.
3. No pooling that survives without family max-t; no cross-market average claim of any kind.
4. No reopening of closed arms, no editing of frozen constants, no exceptions for interesting markets. The kill lines are the product; bending them destroys the only thing SMH uniquely owns, which is the public pre-frozen falsification record.
5. No claim that design-stage replication (GOLD/NIKKEI/DAX gaps positive) proves anything: three positive design gaps raise the prior for the forward test; they cannot substitute for it.

## 6. The honest reading of where the programme stands

NQ's own positive proof (57-of-104) is spent and untouched; its two direction peeks are pending per their register. Cross-market, the mechanism now has four design windows with positive gaps and zero re-tuning, frozen per-market death schedules, and sealed holdouts. If the forward rows confirm at even one new market, the claim "recurrence above climatology under rank-transferred cuts" stops being an NQ fact and becomes a market fact. That is the mould, and the edge route is nothing more than running it forward without bending it.

Literature anchors (for the concepts, not for any claim here): the actuarial object is a survival curve in the Kaplan-Meier (1958) and Cox (1972) tradition; the climatology-matching discipline parallels the conditional-volatility literature (Cont 2001 stylized facts; Clark 1973 subordination; the realized-volatility line through Meddahi-Paranquet-Renault 2012 and the Hawkes/branching line through Filimonov-Sornette 2012 and Ait-Sahalia et al. 2010-2013); the multiplicity discipline is White (2000) reality checks and data-snooping, with Lo (2002) on Sharpe-ratio inference pitfalls; the "design statistics are not proof" discipline is McLean-Pontiff (2016) on post-publication decay; regime-clock economics is Hamilton (1989); the skepticism about long-memory spuriousness is Diebold-Inoue (2001).

# SMH Entry Condition Set v0 (ECS): the theorem boiled down to conditions

Status: DESIGN BOOKKEEPING on the UNSEALED pre-2021 sample only (rule-set-V class). Date filter date < 2021-01-01 throughout; holdout 2021+ SPENT and never touched. Built 12 Nov 2026, session 37, PROGRESS block 45, answering Andrea's directive: develop the framework until it can be boiled down mathematically to a set of conditions that produce the anticipated price change and timely entry. Nothing here is an inferential claim, a test, or a trade; the forward protocol (docs/smh_hazard_v0.md section 5) is unchanged and remains the only path to trust.

Instrument: research/smh_theorem.py (status = the theorem card; selftest = full recomputation from canonical data), wired into verify_all.sh as its 19th check. All constants below are recomputed and asserted by that selftest on every run.

## 1. What this layer adds

The framework's frozen tables (hazard v0, stress clock v0, transitions v0) each carry one piece. This layer assembles them into ONE entry condition set with: (a) uncertainty bands on every headline number, so the claims are intervals, not points; (b) the counterfactual climatology curve, which is what "salience" means operationally: the same 12-week clock started from ANY week, against which the entry condition must earn its name; (c) the timeliness arithmetic, which converts the front-loaded hazard into a precise statement of why the entry act belongs at the cross and what waiting forfeits; (d) the long-run lemma, the honest mathematical form of "inevitable"; and (e) pre-declared forward kill lines, so the occurrence claim can die in public on a date, by count.

## 2. The formal statement

Notation (frozen in the earlier layers): decision week t with sig_w(t) = 8-week trailing weekly log-return std; LOW = 0.01739, HIGH = 0.02697 (frozen pre-2021 tercile cuts). A significant week is |r1| >= 2.5 x sig_w of its decision week. T = the horizon (1..12 weeks) of the FIRST significant week after a clock entry.

THE ENTRY CONDITION SET, compression side (ECS-C). The trigger C is the single measurable condition, evaluated at the decision close:

  C(t): sig_w(t) <= LOW  AND  sig_w(t-1) > LOW   (a cross into compression, n = 41 pre-2021)

The anticipated event E is a significant week within 12 weeks of C. The claims, each with its measured uncertainty:

1. OCCURRENCE. P(T <= 12 | C) = 0.634, Greenwood 95% band [0.487, 0.782]. Counterfactual: P(T <= 12 | any week) = 0.441, band [0.411, 0.471], computed as a Kaplan-Meier of the same 12-week first-event clock started from every pre-2021 decision week with valid sig_w (n = 1050 overlapping episodes; person-week consistency 9807 = 9807 holds). THE BANDS DO NOT OVERLAP: the entry condition's 95% lower bound (0.487) exceeds the climatology's upper bound (0.471). Salience uplift 1.44x on the 12-week clock; at 4 weeks 0.268 vs 0.168. Companion: the compression-state weekly significant-week rate is 0.0969 vs 0.0448 unconditional, rate ratio 2.16, log-band [1.38, 3.39].
2. TIMING. The frozen KM hazard vector is the timing distribution: median lag 5 weeks, cumulative 0.268 by week 4, hazard front-loaded through the h~6 peak (0.148). The clock, not the calendar, carries the "when".
3. DIRECTION: REFUSED IN v0. P(first event up | C) = 17/26 = 0.654, Clopper-Pearson 95% [0.443, 0.828]: the lower bound is below 0.5, so the theorem does not claim a signed change. The anticipated price change is a significant MOVE, not a signed one. The flagged cells (mom12<=0: 6/7 up, CP [0.421, 0.996], SEDUCTIVE; reversal_down: 9/12, [0.428, 0.945]) remain forward-scored hypotheses only, per the standing flag discipline.
4. ABORTS. The condition set dissolves when: the spell ends (sig_w rises above LOW) without an event (median compression dwell 4 weeks; 93% of spells exit to mid; 3/41 jump straight to stress); or 12 quiet weeks pass (the row resolves as a miss, 36.6% of the frozen mass); or the sample ends (censored, KM convention).
5. KILL LINES (pre-declared, forward, occurrence claim p0 = 0.634): the claim dies if, at n scored forward rows, the 12-week event count is at or below:
   n = 10: k <= 3;  n = 20: k <= 8;  n = 40: k <= 19;  n = 60: k <= 31.
   These are one-sided 5% binomial lines. Caveat frozen: forward rows are episode-dependent and the binomial line is an approximation; the binding forward test remains the logged-row Brier/log reliability protocol (52/104 windows, no inferential claim before 104).

The mirror clock (ECS-S) is the same structure on the stress side: trigger sig_w(t) > HIGH after <= HIGH (n = 33); event = de-stress within 12 weeks; P = 0.636, Greenwood 95% [0.472, 0.801], median lag 6; no direction claim exists at all on this side (nothing conditions it, per the stress layer), and its character finding stands: stress decays by calm, not crash (20 of 21 de-stresses without any significant week).

## 3. The timeliness corollary (why the entry act belongs AT the cross)

Because the hazard is front-loaded and the spell is short, delaying the act after a quiet wait forfeits capture probability, computable from the frozen KM:

- Enter at the cross: capture probability within the 12-week window = 0.634.
- Wait 4 quiet weeks, then enter: remaining capture = (0.634 - 0.268)/(1 - 0.268) = 0.500.
- Wait 6 quiet weeks, then enter: remaining capture = (0.634 - 0.439)/(1 - 0.439) = 0.348.

Waiting gains no information the KM does not already condition on (the KM hazards ARE conditional on survival), forfeits the front-loaded mass, and races the spell's own mortality (median dwell 4 weeks). The timely act is therefore defined AT the trigger close, and the forward protocol's rows exist only at entries for exactly this reason. This is the mathematical content of "timely entering": entry timeliness is not a felt skill, it is the difference between 0.634 and 0.348, measured.

## 4. The long-run lemma (the honest form of "inevitable")

The pre-2021 region sequence is an irreducible finite-state chain: every measured link exists (compression to mid 38, mid to compression 37, mid to stress 29, stress to mid 28, stress to compression 4, compression to stress 3; all six directions observed, none absorbing). Irreducible finite chains are recurrent: clock entries recur indefinitely, and each recurrence re-arms the same bounded hazard. Two consequences, stated precisely:

1. ACTUARIAL INEVITABILITY. Across the cycle, significant weeks following compression entries are inevitable in the actuarial sense: the recurrence of the trigger guarantees infinitely many draws from the entry hazard, and the long-run frequency of the anticipated event converges to the entry probability (law of large numbers over the cycle's renewals, with the standing caveat that draws are episode-dependent, so convergence is of the design's idealized kind). This is the same sense in which a life table makes death inevitable and no death dated. The medicine bridge is exact: the SMH is an actuarial table for the market's significant weeks.
2. THE REFUSAL. No dated instance is ever inevitable. A condition set that made the price change inevitable at a date would be the certainty-timing-direction triangle's impossible corner (canvas section 4); the programme's earlier inevitability analysis (session 27, block 35) stands: inevitability with timing and direction does not exist in real time, and any real-time condition for it collapses back into statistics. ECS v0 is that collapse done honestly: one trigger, one event, one timing distribution, bounded probability, pre-declared aborts and kill lines.

So the boiled-down answer to "conditions that inevitably result in the anticipated price change and timely entering" is: the conditions make the HAZARD inevitable to be elevated (bands disjoint from climatology), the ENTRY act timely by a measured 0.634-vs-0.348 margin, and the EVENT recurrent across the cycle; the date and the sign stay refused until forward rows earn them.

## 5. Caveats, frozen with the constants

1. n = 41 compression entries and 33 stress entries in ~20 years; every band above is small-n and the Greenwood/CP constructions assume the episodes are independent draws, which adjacent spells in one regime are not. The disjoint-band salience statement is design bookkeeping under that idealization, not inference.
2. The climatology counterfactual uses 1050 OVERLAPPING episodes (one per decision week); the KM treats them as independent and they are massively overlapping. Its band [0.411, 0.471] is therefore too narrow in truth; this cut favours the salience claim and is flagged here for that reason.
3. The mechanical threshold chase (updated sig_w raises the 2.5-sigma bar as vol expands) remains unseparated from genuine duration conditioning on pre-2021 data; only forward scoring separates them.
4. The kill lines are binomial approximations on episode-dependent rows; they are pre-declared thresholds for public falsification, not calibrated tests, and the 104-window reliability protocol stays binding.
5. All numbers are unsealed pre-2021 design bookkeeping. No claim, no test, no trade, no rule change; the forward protocol, its guards, and the Sunday cadence are untouched.

## 6. Computation log (honest, per programme culture)

The selftest caught the author's hand-guesses before they could freeze: the provisional stress-to-mid chain count (guessed 29, actual 28), the n=60 kill line (guessed 30, actual 31), the climatology cum (guessed 0.146/0.353, actual 0.168/0.441), and the CP band shape (initially two-valued, which broke the comparison; fixed to carry the point estimate). Every embedded constant now equals its recomputation from the canonical data, asserted on each run; the climatology KM passes the standing person-week consistency check (9807 = 9807).

## 7. Addendum (12 Nov 2026, session 62, PROGRESS block 70): the caveat-2 flag is now MEASURED

Caveat 2 above flagged that the climatology counterfactual's Greenwood band [0.411, 0.471] treats 1050 overlapping episodes as independent and is "too narrow in truth". The climatology-counterfactual dependence audit (docs/smh_robustness_v0.md section 10, research/smh_climato_ts_mirror.ts + smh_climato_report.json; TS mirror executed, pandas counterpart pending its first run inside the smh_robust selftest) measured it on the unsealed pre-2021 sample: the rival's SE inflates 0.0154 -> 0.053 (non-overlapping tiles, both phases) -> 0.094 (across 10 two-year era blocks, n_eff ~28 effective eras, 3 of 10 eras already at or above 0.487), and the frozen disjoint-band sentence survives NONE of the three dependence-honest constructions (the entry lower bound 0.487 sits inside every widened band, including the era-mean band's upper edge 0.499). The circular-shift surrogate family was measured as structurally unable to size this pooled statistic (near-degenerate, sd 0.00537, as pre-declared).

CONSEQUENCE, appended without altering any frozen text: the OCCURRENCE item's "THE BANDS DO NOT OVERLAP" sentence is quoted ONLY with BOTH attachments - the definition attachment (it holds in 4 of 11 definition-elasticity cells, section 1 of the robustness doc) and the dependence attachment (0 of 3 dependence-honest rival constructions, section 10). What survives untouched: the occurrence POINT estimates (0.634 vs 0.441), the salience uplift, the gap (positive in every cell of every grid), the kill lines, the calibration expectations, and the forward PAIRED positive proof (57 of 104 matched rows, immune to the rival-band artifact by construction). No frozen constant, probability, protocol or forecast value changed; the frozen definition is unchanged BY FIAT; descriptive design bookkeeping only.

# SMH Event Anatomy v0: the anticipated event dissected, and the two windows

Status: DESIGN BOOKKEEPING on the UNSEALED pre-2021 sample only (rule-set-V class). Date filter date < 2021-01-01 throughout; holdout 2021+ SPENT and never touched. Built 12 Nov 2026, session 39, PROGRESS block 47, on Andrea's "continue developing". Nothing here is an inferential claim, a test, or a trade; the forward protocol is unchanged. All constants below are recomputed and asserted by the smh_theorem.py selftest, which is verify_all's 19th check.

This layer dissects E itself, the anticipated event of the entry condition set, and in doing so it resolves a real internal tension in ECS v0 that the person-week machinery could not see: the forecast clock is ENTRY-anchored, but the theorem's abort condition ("the set dissolves when the spell ends") is SPELL-anchored, and compression spells are short (median dwell 4 weeks) while the event median lag is 5. Those two conventions could not both be right about the same number. The competing-risks decomposition below is the resolution, and it is this layer's main finding.

## 1. Competing risks: the spell versus the event (n = 41 compression entries)

For each pre-2021 compression entry, two clocks start together: the spell's own mortality (the first week the region escapes compression) and the first significant week. Classification of the 41 episodes:

| class | count | share | meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| event strictly in-spell (h < spell end) | 7 | 0.171 | the event arrives while the region still reads compression |
| event AT the escape (h = spell end) | 11 | 0.268 | the event IS the week the spell dies; the significant week pushes sig_w out of the cut |
| event after the escape (h > spell end) | 8 | 0.195 | the region escaped first; the armed clock keeps running anyway |
| no event within 12 weeks | 15 | 0.366 | the scored miss of the frozen table |

Three consequences, now frozen into the theorem card:

1. THE SPELL DIES OF ITS EVENT. The single largest realization class (11 of 26 events, 42%) is the event that ends the compression spell itself. The most common way a quiet spell ends is not a drift out on quiet weeks; it is the anticipated significant week, the expansion the clock was armed to catch. This is the mechanism-level reading of law 1 (vol mean-reversion) at the entry grain, and it is the survival-analysis form of the wave practitioner's "expansion out of compression": measured, and mostly it is the event itself that does the expanding.
2. THE TWO WINDOWS, DISTINGUISHED. The FORECAST window is entry-anchored: the logged row resolves at 12 weeks regardless of what the region does, and its probability is the frozen 0.634. The ACT window is what a practitioner holds who dissolves at spell end: capture 18/41 = 0.439 (7 strictly in-spell + 11 at-escape). The gap, 0.195 (8 events), is the post-escape mass: events that arrive after the region read has already changed. Of those 8, five follow spells that lasted only 1-2 weeks (the flash exits: the spell dies early on quiet drift, the clock keeps running, the event comes later). ECS v0's abort item is hereby refined, on the record: dissolving the act at spell end is a legitimate choice with a measured cost (0.439 vs 0.634); the forecast row itself never dissolves early. No protocol change: the forward rows were always entry-anchored; what was wrong was only the suggestion that the act and the row share a window.
3. THE ARMED-CLOCK INTERPRETATION IS NOW MEASURED, not assumed. 31% of the event mass arrives after the region has escaped, so the compression cross is genuinely a trigger that arms a process outliving the region read. The alternative reading (the event requires the region to persist) is refuted by the design sample: 8 of 26 events happened with the region already elsewhere.

## 2. The volume signature (law 4 measured at the entry grain)

Volume ratio = week volume / median of the trailing 8 weeks (min 4), the same basis as the canonical thin_vol_flag. Pre-2021 significant weeks (all 47 of them; note 47/1050 = 0.0448, the unconditional rate, exact):

- significant weeks: median ratio 1.138, versus 1.004 for all weeks;
- 70.2% of significant weeks are above their trailing baseline, versus 50.7% for all weeks;
- ZERO of the 47 event weeks are thin-flagged.

The framework's law 4 (impact is concave in volume; effort must confirm result) is confirmed at the weekly grain in the design sample: the anticipated event comes with effort attached, and a thin event week essentially does not occur. The 26 compression-entry first events specifically: median ratio 1.124, mean 1.162. CAVEAT, frozen: holiday-week distortion is real but small (5 of the 26 event weeks had fewer than 5 sessions); volume here is the weekly proxy, far from order flow (the standing broker-export item); no causal claim, no rule change.

## 3. The size of the event: overshoot

The event bar is |r1| >= 2.5 x sig_w of the decision week. The realized first events overshoot their own bar: median 1.41x, maximum 2.63x. The median significant week is therefore a ~3.5-sigma week. This anchors what "anticipated price change" concretely means in ECS v0: not a point target, a threshold-crossing event whose measured central size is about three and a half weekly sigmas.

## 4. The direction-volume asymmetry (flagged, anatomy not direction)

Splitting the 26 first events by sign: up events (17) carry median volume ratio 0.938, BELOW their trailing baseline; down events (9) carry 1.733, nearly double it. The equity-index asymmetry, VSA-style: liquidation comes on effort, rallies grind on thinning tape. FLAGGED per the standing discipline: n = 17/9, selected after seeing the table, direction is refused in ECS v0 and this is anatomy rather than a direction claim; absolutely untrustworthy until forward-scored. Recorded because it is the concrete weekly-grain shape of the effort-vs-result relation the canon documents on order-flow data.

## 5. Caveats, frozen

1. All classes are small-n (41 entries, 26 events); the competing-risks shares inherit episode dependence.
2. The volume signature is a weekly-proxy measurement; effort-vs-result on true order flow remains data-blocked (the broker/CME export standing item). No VXN comparison exists yet, so "unseen" remains untested.
3. The overshoot distribution inherits the mechanical threshold chase (the bar itself moves with sig_w).
4. All numbers are unsealed pre-2021 design bookkeeping. No claim, no test, no trade; no protocol, rule, or guard change; the forward rows stay entry-anchored and empty until the market's next clock entry.

# SMH Programme Frontier v0 (the self-selection layer)

Written 19 Nov 2026 from the record through session 107. This file is the direction-selection layer for scheduled and autonomous sessions of the AVFI/SMH weekly programme. It is the layer-2 implementation of the autonomy structure: sessions do not need the owner present to choose what to advance. This file does NOT govern the parallel crypto-L2 deep-read lineage (that lineage records its own conventions in its own log; its content carries zero status here and is never touched from here).

The frontier implements task selection. Goal re-ranking (layer 3) is proposal-form only: any session may append a re-ranking proposal with its reasoning; the ranking itself changes only by the owner's word at the weekly directorate, unless the change is a pure factual status update of a front the record already determines.

## Session contract

1. Read the tail of the programme record (/home/user/knowledge/trading-research/KNOWLEDGE.md) first. The record is the state; never act on remembered state alone.
2. Read this file. Claim the top unclaimed lawful front by appending a dated CLAIM line under that front in the frontier log below. One session, one claimed front.
3. Execute one concrete step within the front's rules: small increments, real command output in the record, never a claim of working without execution.
4. Append the outcome (frontier log entry plus a numbered session entry in the record; append-only; numbering plain from the tail; concurrent sessions never renumber or overwrite).
5. Register any new front discovered (entry criteria, what it feeds, its own death condition). A proposed front containing anything from the gate list is refused at registration, and the refusal is logged.

If no lawful unclaimed front exists, run the stewardship baseline (F2) and log "no lawful front" honestly. An honest empty session is a valid session. The frontier must never manufacture work to appear productive.

## The gate list (absolute; restated from the record)

A scheduled session may NOT: evaluate or descriptively read the spent NQ 2021+ holdout; run the original battery or the stage2b H8/H9 scripts; compute on any sealed 2021+ window (NQ holdout, GOLD/NIKKEI/DAX, BTC); arm, ratify or execute any amendment (unsigned drafts stay unevaluated); change any kill line, frozen constant, registered default, rule set, protocol or observation horizon; re-tune anything after a null; claim or score direction; recommend live trading; treat a backtest as evidence; rewind or rewrite record history; touch the parallel lineage's tasks or logs. Unknown cases: refuse and log the refusal. The two load-bearing rules do not change with any amount of autonomy: the default assumption is that there is no edge, and the burden of proof is on every signal.

## Ranked fronts

F1 (rank 1, ACTIVE, recurring) Forward-row production and track health.
Entry: every session checks the three standing tracks (stage 7 weekly cadence, SMH forward protocol, macro Amendment 9 Sunday booking) for health: the just-completed booking, the arm state of each scheduled task, the logs' append-only integrity.
Feeds: the proof object (kill lines, calibration expectations, the 57-of-104 positive proof). Owner of the proof: forward time, nobody else.
Death: none. This front is the programme.
Standing sub-items: the Sunday cadence automation task (created 19 Nov 2026); the macro first fire (2026-11-22 23:45 Malta); the V4h booking-mechanism flag (owner's task-system check only; the engine is never run from helper sessions).

F2 (rank 2, ACTIVE, recurring) Stewardship and verification.
Entry: any session, at minimum on every environment reset: rebuild the tree from the bundle, verify_all (25 checks), the read-only v4h audit, record integrity per the mount rules (a possibly-intact record is never rebuilt over).
Feeds: reproducibility credibility; the record's evidential value.
Death: none.

F3 (rank 3, ACTIVE, standing rule) Website honesty pass.
Entry: whenever the record tail moves: the site (pipeline/docs/site.html) narrative must carry the previous session's state; check counts (KPI number of automated checks, verdict rows) against reality; sync both trees; diff-verify.
Feeds: the public falsification record; the honest-negative-results checklist.
Standing rule: the site never lags the record tail by more than one session entry.
Death: none.

F4 (rank 4, ACTIVE) Decision-queue hygiene.
Entry: whenever a signature-queue item changes state or a new draft is authored: audit the queue doc against the record, citation-audit any new draft before it reaches the owner (the session-87/88 precedent), keep every pending decision with where its draft lives and what signing and refusing each do.
Feeds: the owner's decision quality; batch-ratification efficiency.
Death: none.

F5 (rank 5, ACTIVE, daily) Owner-decay watch.
Entry: every session ends with a three-line state of the decay items (the L2 download, the V4h task check, pending signatures, library re-uploads: Bouchaud full text, Jäckel, the 2011 Harmonic Elliott Wave): unchanged / changed / resolved by the owner. Observation only; no nagging artefacts, no duplicates of his mechanisms.
Feeds: the only two items in the programme that rot with delay.
Death: none.

F6 (rank 6, GATED on the standing order) Descriptive-audit frontier (the generative layer).
GATE: this front is lawful only while /docs/standing_order_autonomy.md reads SIGNED. While unsigned, sessions run F1-F5 only.
Entry: a new self-audit of ALREADY-REGISTERED machinery (a definition-elasticity grid, a dependence audit, a selftest-widening, a parity mirror, an anchor extension), pre-declared in the frontier log BEFORE any computation: the audited object, the one knob, the all-cells-reported commitment, the none-selected commitment.
Rules: audits only; unsealed pre-2021 data only; no forward-return rule evaluation; no re-tuning of any frozen quantity; no new registered claims; the audit adds no evidence, it bounds an existing claim; if the design record already declared a stronger rule (an explicit not-proposed list item), the audit is refused.
Feeds: robustness honesty of the standing claims.
Death (per audit and per front): any audit that selects a cell, proposes a rule change, or touches a sealed window dies on discovery and the death is logged. Three dead audits close the front pending the owner's word.

F7 (rank 7, ACTIVE, zero-status output) Drafting front.
Entry: when the record surfaces a question needing the owner's word with no draft existing: author the unsigned draft per the house pattern (pre-registration, frozen defaults, power readout before sign-off, both paths recorded).
Feeds: his decision stack. Output carries zero status by construction.
Death: none. Live drafts: D-1 (accrual readout line), D-6 (tree-relative repo resolution); candidates A13/C-W1 and A14/D-3 already drafted and citation-audited.

F8 (rank 8, BLOCKED, keep-ready) Data-unlock preparation.
Entry: only runbook and gate-script upkeep for the standing unlocks (the L2 download per LOCAL_DEPLOY.md and the l2 runbook; the broker/CME export for Amendment 10; the options feed for Amendment 14). No computation on data that does not exist; nothing runs before its dated signed amendment.
Feeds: the speed of the moment each unlock arrives.
Death: none; the fronts unblock on his hands, not on sessions.
